Takes the object identifier, temperature-modifying receptacle, and final target receptacle as inputs, and outputs the object at the target location with its temperature state changed. ([zjunlp/Skills](https://github.com/zjunlp/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-collections/alfworld/alfworld-temperature-regulator) · ★8)
- **alfworld-tool-locator**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/data/alfworld-tool-locator) · ★745 · 2 copies)
- **alfworld-tool-locator**: This skill searches for a specified tool or device (e.g., a desklamp) within the environment by checking relevant surfaces. It should be triggered when the agent needs a tool to interact with another object as part of the task. The skill takes a tool name as implicit input and outputs navigation actions to likely storage spots (e.g., sidetables, shelves) until the tool is found. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/tools/alfworld-tool-locator) · ★67 · 4 copies)
- **alfworld-tool-locator**: Searches for a specified tool or device (e.g., a desklamp, knife, or sponge) within the ALFWorld environment by checking relevant surfaces. Use when you need a tool to interact with another object as part of a task but the tool is not in your inventory or immediate vicinity. Takes a tool name as implicit input and navigates to likely storage spots (sidetables, shelves, countertops) until the tool is found. ([zjunlp/Skills](https://github.com/zjunlp/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-collections/alfworld/alfworld-tool-locator) · ★8)
- **alfworld-tool-user**: Use when the agent needs to apply a tool to a target object in ALFWorld to accomplish an interaction such as cleaning, heating, cooling, or examining. This skill handles locating both the tool and target object, then executing the correct environment action (e.g., `clean`, `heat`, `cool`, `use`) to progress the task. ([zjunlp/Skills](https://github.com/zjunlp/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-collections/alfworld/alfworld-tool-user) · ★8)
- **alg-admin-api**: Use when AI/脚本管理 alg DB (create/update/del/reorder/batch-validate/dedup) 免 OAuth。X-Admin-Key 通道 + 4 个 admin endpoints + 直 SQL playbook。Triggers: "alg admin", "X-Admin-Key", "reorder cases", "del alg case", "alg dup", "批量校验 alg", "AdminCaseEditor", "alg_cases SQL". - **alg-optimize**: 算法题 TLE（超时）定位与优化：先算复杂度再动手，给出根源分析与可落地的优化方案。用户刷题遇到超时、要求优化算法、分析复杂度时使用。 ([suxiangyu138/Java-AI-LLM-Learning-Records](https://github.com/suxiangyu138/Java-AI-LLM-Learning-Records/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/alg-optimize) · ★7)

- **Alga PSA API Patterns**: Alga PSA MCP fundamentals through Conduit: the X-Alga-Instance-Url / X-Alga-Api-Key / X-Alga-Tenant-Id headers, the 42-tool read-only surface (no writes, nothing admin-gated), the four pagination styles (page/limit, limit-only search, offset, cursor), response truncation, error messages, concurrency and retry limits, and the untrusted-content envelope. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/alga-psa/alga-psa/skills/api-patterns) · ★55)
- **Alga PSA Client Review**: Building a per-client account picture from Alga PSA for a QBR, renewal or health check: client record, locations and contacts, ticket volume and open backlog over a period, time spent, invoices and payment state, contracts, assets, and active projects. Covers which Alga filters exist per record type (contracts have no client filter) and how to keep internal notes out of client-facing output. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/alga-psa/alga-psa/skills/client-review) · ★55)
- **Alga PSA Project Status**: Reading Alga PSA projects for status reporting: projects by client, status or search; phases; tasks per project or phase; the ordered task status columns (project_status_mapping_id) a project or phase uses; and time logged against project tasks. Covers that the project status filter takes a status UUID, not a name. Read-only. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/alga-psa/alga-psa/skills/project-status) · ★55)
- **Alga PSA Ticket Triage**: Reading the Alga PSA service desk: open queues by board, status, priority and assignee; stale, unassigned and aging tickets; a single ticket's full history (comments with internal notes, logged time, linked assets); finding the ticket behind an RMM alert by external link; and knowledge articles that match a problem. Read-only: proposes ticket changes, never makes them. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/alga-psa/alga-psa/skills/ticket-triage) · ★55)
- **Alga PSA Time and Billing**: Alga PSA time and billing reads: time sheet approval states and the backlog waiting for a manager, time entries by technician, work item type, service and billable flag, technician schedules, invoice status (draft, sent, overdue, paid ...) with date-range filters, invoice line items, and the service catalog. Read-only: approvals and invoice actions stay in Alga. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/alga-psa/alga-psa/skills/time-and-billing) · ★55)

- **algebra-master**: 代数大师 (Algebra Master) —— Google DeepMind《Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms》四种领域人格之一「代数」的专家顾问。以群论/Galois、交换代数、李代数与根系、表示论、同调代数为主干，附带范畴论与几何/拓扑/K 理论接口。给出代数问题的人格化思考方式、常用策略与陷阱、知识地图与可计算结论。触发词："群"、"环"、"域"、"理想"、"模"、"Galois"、"Sylow"、"特征标"、"表示"、"Lie 代数"、"根系"、"Dynkin"、"最高权"、"同调"、"上同调"、"导出函子"、"Tor"、"Ext"、"谱序列"、"范畴"、"伴随"、"Yoneda"、"普适性质"、"ideal"、"module"、"representation"、"root system"、"homology"、"adjunction"。 ([aresbit/AutoAgent](https://github.com/aresbit/AutoAgent/tree/HEAD/packages/opencc/src/tools/AlgebraMasterTool/assets) · ★4)

- **algebra-of-record**: PROACTIVELY load when implementing a new numerical method, building a verification-grade reference solver, or reviewing whether a derivation→code pipeline meets project discipline. Codifies the SymPy-as-canonical-algebra pattern: derivations bifurcate at a specialized form into Branch 1 (continuous reference: SymPy, SymPy+mpmath, or MMS) and Branch 2 (discretized production: numpy/scipy). Branch 2 is verified at L1 by cross-check against Branch 1. Sphinx narrates both, with the SymPy module as the canonical algebra-of-record. Read by sub-agents that build, document, or verify reference solver… ([deOliveira-R/ORPHEUS](https://github.com/deOliveira-R/ORPHEUS/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/algebra-of-record) · ★1)

- **algebraic-mind-vacoa**: How to Build Marcus's Algebraic Mind: Algebro-Deterministic Substrate over Galois Fields (arXiv:2605.21379). Maps Gary Marcus's three pillars of cognitive architecture (operations over variables, recursively structured representations, individual/kind distinction) onto the PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl hyperdimensional computing architecture. Uses XOR-and-shift over GF(2) as a single algebraic primitive. Activation: vacoal, hyperdimensional computing, algebraic mind, Gary Marcus, cognitive architecture, reversible variable binding, compositional bundling, Galois fields, PyVaCoAl, counterfactual reasoning. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/math-statistics/algebraic-mind-vacoa) · ★4 · 4 copies)
- **algebraic-modelling**: Algebraic modelling — find properties (associativity, identity, idempotency, …) and structures (monoids, semilattices, functors) in the domain, then derive implementation and tests from the laws.
